- **mathmodel-methods**: 数学建模方法库与选型技能：按问题族（评价/排序、优化、预测、分类聚类、微分方程、仿真、信号处理、前沿创新）给出候选算法、适用边界、实现入口与验证方式，覆盖基础数值、经典优化、图论网络、群体智能、机器学习、统计与数据挖掘、综合评价、物理工程建模与前沿方法，并附候选对比表模板、问题递进关系与常用库速查。当用户问"这题用什么算法""推荐建模方法""算法选型对比""某类问题怎么建模求解"时使用；论文写作、排版、出图、去 AI 与评分分别改用 mathmodel-paper / mathmodel-figure / mathmodel-diagram / mathmodel-deai / mathmodel-score。 ([Escap1ng/mathmodel-studio](https://github.com/Escap1ng/mathmodel-studio/tree/HEAD/skills/mathmodel-methods) · ★7 · 2 copies)
- **mathmodel-orchestrator**: 数学建模多 Agent 编排与异构模型复核。用于长上下文赛题、需要把题意、文献、建模、求解、写作和验收分给隔离 Agent，或需要 Codex 主控联合 DeepSeek 等 OpenAI 兼容模型做独立审查时；生成哈希固定的 CONTEXT_PACKET、AGENT_RESULT、分歧报告和事件日志，但不允许 AI 自批阶段门禁。 ([ShuoSachiko/MathMN](https://github.com/ShuoSachiko/MathMN/tree/HEAD/skills/mathmodel-orchestrator) · ★1)
- **mathmodel-paper**: 数学建模论文写作与排版技能：论文写作规范（结构、摘要五段式、模型建立与求解、公式、模型评价、附录、正文语言表述）+ LaTeX 骨架与页面设置、匿名红线 + pandoc 转 Word 与 python-docx 版式微调 + 参考文献著录与查证（GB/T 7714）。当用户要写论文、要论文模板或骨架、写摘要、按竞赛口径排版、生成或微调 Word 论文、处理参考文献时触发。赛题分析、算法选择与代码实现改用 mathmodel-core / mathmodel-methods；去 AI 与零宽字符清理改用 mathmodel-deai；结构自检与评分改用 mathmodel-score；数据图表与示意图改用 mathmodel-figure / mathmodel-diagram。 ([Escap1ng/mathmodel-studio](https://github.com/Escap1ng/mathmodel-studio/tree/HEAD/skills/mathmodel-paper) · ★7 · 2 copies)
- **mathmodel-paper-workflow**: 数学建模竞赛论文端到端总编排（CUMCM 国赛、MCM/APMCM 美赛、华为杯、华中、五邑、统计建模等）。当用户说“写一篇数模论文”“做这道建模题”“从建模到论文走完整流程”“帮我把这篇竞赛论文跑完”时使用。按阶段调度 mma-paper、paper-search、data-search、paper-diagram、mathmodel-figure-templates、mma-review，产出可提交的 LaTeX/PDF、配图与评审报告。 ([2145602879/codex-mathmodel-paper](https://github.com/2145602879/codex-mathmodel-paper/tree/HEAD/plugins/mathmodel-paper/skills/mathmodel-paper-workflow) · ★1)

- **mathmodel-score**: 论文评分与质量自检模块：按百分制五维（摘要 30 / 算法模型 20 / 创新性 20 / 写作 15 / 排版 15）对论文打分，交付前逐项自检并给出扣分明细、优化轮次与达标判定。用 score_card.py 把评分卡 JSON 校验并渲染成评分表（达标线 85，一票否决直接淘汰，熔断 3 轮）。当用户要求给论文打分、自检、生成评分表、评估能否交付、判断 AI/合规风险时触发。建模与写作规范以主技能 mathmodel-core 为准，版式与去 AI 分别见 mathmodel-paper / mathmodel-deai。 ([Escap1ng/mathmodel-studio](https://github.com/Escap1ng/mathmodel-studio/tree/HEAD/skills/mathmodel-score) · ★7 · 2 copies)
- **mathmodel-scoring-gate**: 数学建模的可复算评分门禁。把一份 5 维 critic JSON 变成加权分与七档 verdict（block/pass_early/pass/pass_with_review/refine/refine_partial/carryover），写进 state/decision_log.json，供 M1/P1/P2/W1/W2 门禁与 CUMCM/MCM/电工杯阶段放行使用。触发词：评分、打分、verdict、门禁判定、加权评分、够不够格、是否放行、critic 回执、per-Qi 聚合、子问合并。 ([momoqiqi-qwq/math-modeling-skills](https://github.com/momoqiqi-qwq/math-modeling-skills/tree/HEAD/mathmodel-scoring-gate))

- **mathnet**: Guidance for Math.NET Numerics library for .NET. - **mathnet-retrieve-eval**: Probes a model's ability to retrieve mathematically equivalent problems from a large corpus using embeddings. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MathNet-Retrieve, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Recall@k. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MathNet-Solve, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Problem Solving Accuracy. - **mathprompt**: MathPrompt symbolic-math jailbreak (arXiv:2409.11445). Re-encode a natural-language harmful instruction into a symbolic math problem (set theory + abstract algebra + symbolic logic) via an attacker LLM with a verbatim system prompt + 2 few-shot demos, prepend a "solve then give a real-world example with real entities" instruction, and send to the target LLM. Judge = HarmBench classifier (verbatim prompt). Black-box, no GPU. Use to test whether a target's safety alignment generalizes to symbolic mathematics. ([fresh-ma/RedDarwin](https://github.com/fresh-ma/RedDarwin/tree/HEAD/XSkillLib/Single-turn/Code-Encapsulation/2409.11445_MathPrompt/mathprompt) · ★3)
- **mathprompt2**: XSkillForge single-skill-evolution proposal mathprompt2, derived from mathprompt. Use when inspecting, running, comparing, or evaluating this proposal and its evidence. ([fresh-ma/RedDarwin](https://github.com/fresh-ma/RedDarwin/tree/HEAD/XSkillLib/Proposal/mathprompt2) · ★3)
- **mathproof-skill**: Construct, audit, explain, or research mathematical proofs with correctness-first reasoning. Use when solving proof problems; checking a submitted proof; locating the first unsupported step; finding missing assumptions or counterexamples; explaining a proof pedagogically; researching named, recent, or uncertain theorems; or formalizing and machine-checking a claim, proof, or counterexample in Lean 4 with Mathlib. ([Ztang-Yit-Xiaang/mathproof-skill](https://github.com/Ztang-Yit-Xiaang/mathproof-skill/tree/HEAD/skill) · ★1)
- **mathproofs-claw**: Skill for interacting with the Lean-Claw Arena to prove math theorems using Lean 4. ([xdelin/OpenClaw-Education-Skills](https://github.com/xdelin/OpenClaw-Education-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/mathproofs-claw) · ★2)
- **mathqa-python-eval**: Evaluates a model's ability to translate complex mathematical word problems into executable Python code that computes a specific numerical answer. It probes semantic grounding of natural language, arithmetic reasoning, and straight-line code generation. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MathQA-Python, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mathqa-python-eval) · ★3)
- **mathreader-memory**: Memory protocol for the math-literature-reader plugin. Two-layer split: top-level project memory stays CLEAN (only paper index + user-defined keywords); per-paper .mathreader/ subdirectory holds all work state (indexes, checkpoints, state.json, diagrams, notation, dep-graph, cross-ref records, Stacks matches). Triggered when .mathreader/, checkpoint, resume, state.json, or a previously-loaded paper is referenced. Activated by source-resolver on follow-up turns about an existing paper. ([Ou-Liu-Red-Sugar/math-literature-reader](https://github.com/Ou-Liu-Red-Sugar/math-literature-reader/tree/HEAD/skills/mathreader-memory) · ★2)
- **mathreal-eval**: Evaluates the ability of multimodal large language models to perform K-12 mathematical reasoning on real-world, mobile-captured images. It probes robustness to visual degradation (blur, rotation, handwritten annotations) and perspective variations, measuring how well models extract text and figures to solve math problems under imperfect conditions. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MathReal, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Loose Accuracy (Acc). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/mathreal-eval) · ★3)
- **mathrsi-assemble**: 将原题、已证明引理、准确文献进口与计算证据装配成完整数学证明，调用 ARIS proof-writer，检查实际输入与条件引理的连接。用于已有足够结果后形成现用正文；正式审计的新稿可直接写 PROOF.tex，完成或发现缺口后返回原调用方。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-assemble) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-degree-diameter**: Research finite simple undirected degree–diameter graphs using actual graph constructions, shared route coverage, frozen-neighborhood transfer and scoped obstruction certificates. Use for a new or resumed degree–diameter task, selecting a domain method or connecting an existing result to the original graph; historical archive state never resumes a Goal. ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/plugins/degree-diameter/skills/mathrsi-degree-diameter) · ★54 · 2 copies)
- **mathrsi-ecc**: 为二元线性纠错码的构造与不存在性研究适配矩阵、归约、分类、陪集联合条件和具体核验；区分单个见证验收与全对象域排除。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/plugins/ecc/skills/mathrsi-ecc) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-handoff**: 将数学研究的原题、可读证明和真实验证证据整理成可复现交付与恢复入口，按需求调用 ARIS render-html、引用审计或编译，准备独立目录复现材料、最终 wiki 与 NEXT_SESSION。支持准确部分交接，不重开已闭数学或把展示成功当证明。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-handoff) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-happy-ending**: 研究Erdős–Szekeres多边形与一般有序rank-3 signotope受限容量N(m,r,s)（m=7–10及一般m），执行同输入扇区缺额、全倒二点补偿、状态基数、坐标切片双计数、共享标签核、原生边界迹对偶、共同匹配替锁与合法chart运输；给base返回准确证明、反例或未闭义务，区分已复验有限证书、结构工作稿与平面几何实现。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/plugins/happy-ending/skills/mathrsi-happy-ending) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-hypercube-extremal**: Develop and verify hypercube extremal bounds (finite ex(Q_n, C_4) and asymptotic density) using common local completions, synchronised rooted flags, COP/degree-potential certificates, same-graph multi-measure finite pullbacks, and primal–dual coevolution (cube_coevolve). Use for cube forbidden-subgraph research or consuming Erdős 86 Q7/Q8/asymptotic assets; returns scoped mathematics and checks to the calling MathRSI workflow. ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/plugins/hypercube-extremal/skills/mathrsi-hypercube-extremal) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-hypercube-hamilton**: Prove and verify Hamilton path/cycle extension of prescribed hypercube matchings using root-preserving reductions, universal connectivity-rank forests, maximal-domain positive certificates and marked gluing. Use for Ruskey–Savage-type research, controlled auxiliary pairings, finite universal plans, same-input coverage costs, maximal-matching closure and scoped Q6/Q7 proof assets. ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/plugins/hypercube-hamilton/skills/mathrsi-hypercube-hamilton) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-meta-evolve**: Evolve an existing MathRSI version from real mathematical research evidence. Distill successful and failed work into base changes, domain plugins, task-local knowledge or deferred proposals; implement, use and reconcile the actual changes. Use for MathRSI iteration or absorbing a completed research project, not for silently changing a running theorem-solving workflow. ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-meta-evolve) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-prepare**: 数学研究的准备子工作流，实际串联原题初步澄清、广泛文献与反例勘误调查、正式规格定稿及wiki恢复。已有研究按真实断点进入后继阶段，不重复从零调查。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-prepare) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-propertyb**: 为有限一致超图的 Property B 研究提供归约、真实迹、共同坏对、概率界与核验适配；与 MathRSI base 配合推进具体下界或反例任务。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/plugins/propertyb/skills/mathrsi-propertyb) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-prove**: 沿选定数学路线实际推进证明、构造和计算义务，调用推导与证明技能、领域程序及独占worker，立即回收核验并接回原题。用于从路线报告产生完整候选证明或精确缺口；换对象或方法交给 mathrsi-route-loop，最终装配验收交给 mathrsi-verify。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-prove) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-research**: 端到端数学研究主工作流，实际串联问题准备与文献、求解路线对抗迭代、证明推进、完整装配验证和可复现交付。组合本仓MathRSI与ARIS技能及领域插件，遇新障碍定向回文献/路线，复用wiki与证据，按需Lean。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-research) · ★54 · 2 copies)
- **mathrsi-route-loop**: 执行ARIS auto-review-loop的数学路线适配，准备具体方法，经真实research-review、持续reviewer memory、实际推导或探针、修复复核与逐轮恢复，交付可执行求解路线和未证义务。用于选择或改造证明方法及重判卡住的路线；完整证明验收交给mathrsi-verify。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/plugins/degree-diameter/archive/dd-latest-two-parameters-20261116/framework_snapshot/base/skills/mathrsi-route-loop) · ★54 · 2 copies)
- **mathrsi-specify**: 整理数学研究的初步问题或恢复既有任务，并在文献调查后定稿原对象、量词、参数、依赖与验收。用于新题起步、原题含糊或已有规格需要对齐；不负责替用户换题或评选求解路线。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-specify) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-survey**: 调查数学问题的异名、等价表述、已有结论与反例勘误，深读关键原文形成可调用的数学进口和准确基线，并持续接回任务wiki。用于新题广搜、换参数或方法后的查漏及文献断点恢复；不以查新分数替代证明或自行更改原目标。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-survey) · ★54)
- **mathrsi-verify**: 对已装配数学证明或明确局部义务执行验证子工作流：按需装配，核覆盖与独立精确复算，调用 ARIS proof-checker 自身修复循环，并在需要时接 Lean。输出准确范围的 VERIFICATION.md；实质数学缺口返回 mathrsi-prove，不用路线评分充当定理验收。 ([lisresearchgroup/math-rsi](https://github.com/lisresearchgroup/math-rsi/tree/HEAD/base/skills/mathrsi-verify) · ★54)
